import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_2/round_0"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = set(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(sorted(valid_iids), 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx[x] for x in seq if x in item2idx]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(df):
    unique_uids = sorted(df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 50
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test

    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items

    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=200)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 300

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth")))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                    uid_probs[uid] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    for uid, probs in uid_probs.items():
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": str(uid), "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": str(uid), "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = [{"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(r["prediction"].split(",")[:10])} for r in top100_rows]
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

[Worker-2] 🔥 执行冒烟测试...
[Worker-1] 生成的训练代码为:

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict
import csv

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/'
OUT_DIR = './model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_1/round_0'
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'train.csv'))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'test.csv'))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'item.csv'))

item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for iid in item_df['iid'].values:
    if iid not in item2idx:
        item2idx[iid] = len(item2idx)
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
num_items = len(item2idx)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'item2idx.pkl'), 'wb') as f:
    pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'idx2item.pkl'), 'wb') as f:
    pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def parse_seq(seq_str):
    if pd.isna(seq_str): return []
    s = str(seq_str).strip()
    if s.startswith('[') and s.endswith(']'):
        s = s[1:-1]
    return [x.strip() for x in s.split(',') if x.strip()]

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df):
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 50
EMBED_DIM = 128
NUM_LAYERS = 2
NUM_HEADS = 4
BATCH_SIZE = 256
MAX_EPOCHS = 200
PATIENCE = 40
TTA_ROUNDS = 30
DEVICE = 'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'

class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_list'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_iid'].astype(str).tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.uids)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = str(self.uids[idx])
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        seq_idx = [item2idx.get(i, 0) for i in seq if item2idx.get(i, 0) != 0]
        if len(seq_idx) > MAX_LEN:
            seq_idx = seq_idx[-MAX_LEN:]
        pad_len = MAX_LEN - len(seq_idx)
        seq_idx = [0] * pad_len + seq_idx
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq_idx, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        else:
            target = self.targets[idx]
            target_idx = item2idx.get(target, 0)
            return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(target_idx, dtype=torch.long), uid

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
test_ds = RecDataset(test_df, is_test=True)

train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, embed_dim, max_len, num_layers, num_heads, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, embed_dim, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, embed_dim)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([
            nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
                d_model=embed_dim, nhead=num_heads, dim_feedforward=embed_dim*4,
                dropout=dropout, batch_first=True, activation='gelu'
            ) for _ in range(num_layers)
        ])
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(embed_dim)
        self.apply(self._init_weights)

    def _init_weights(self, module):
        if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
            torch.nn.init.xavier_uniform_(module.weight)
            if module.bias is not None:
                module.bias.data.zero_()
        elif isinstance(module, nn.Embedding):
            torch.nn.init.normal_(module.weight, mean=0.0, std=0.02)

    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        attn_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(seq.size(1), seq.size(1), device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x, src_mask=attn_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        x = self.norm(x)
        out = x[:, -1, :]
        logits = torch.matmul(out, self.item_emb.weight.T)
        return logits

model = SASRec(num_items, EMBED_DIM, MAX_LEN, NUM_LAYERS, NUM_HEADS).to(DEVICE)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=MAX_EPOCHS)

def compute_ndcg10(targets, scores, k=10):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i, t in enumerate(targets):
        if t == 0: continue
        row_scores = scores[i].copy()
        row_scores[0] = -1e9
        topk_idx = np.argsort(row_scores)[::-1][:k]
        if t in topk_idx:
            rank = np.where(topk_idx == t)[0][0] + 1
            ndcg += 1.0 / np.log2(rank + 1)
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience_cnt = 0
best_model_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'best_model.pth')

for epoch in range(1, MAX_EPOCHS + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    num_batches = 0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(DEVICE), target.to(DEVICE)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item()
        num_batches += 1
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / max(num_batches, 1)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        val_targets = []
        val_scores = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq = seq.to(DEVICE)
                logits = model(seq)
                val_scores.append(logits.cpu().numpy())
                val_targets.extend(target.numpy().tolist())
        val_scores = np.vstack(val_scores)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(val_targets, val_scores)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Loss: {avg_loss:.4f} | Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")
        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), best_model_path)
            print(f"  -> Best model saved. NDCG: {best_ndcg:.4f}")
        else:
            patience_cnt += 10
            if patience_cnt >= PATIENCE:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

model.load_state_dict(torch.load(best_model_path))
model.to(DEVICE)

def generate_outputs(loader, out_prefix):
    model.train()
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(num_items))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    for _ in range(TTA_ROUNDS):
        for batch in loader:
            if len(batch) == 2:
                seq, uids = batch
            else:
                seq, _, uids = batch
            seq = seq.to(DEVICE)
            with torch.no_grad():
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1).cpu().numpy()
            for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                uid_str = str(uid)
                uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    rows_top10 = []
    rows_top100 = []
    rows_softmax = []

    for uid in sorted(uid_probs.keys()):
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        probs[0] = -1.0
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        iids = []
        scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item.get(idx)
            if iid:
                iids.append(iid)
                scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(iids) >= 100: break

        pred_10 = ",".join(iids[:10])
        pred_100 = ",".join(iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{sc:.4f}" for iid, sc in zip(iids, scores)])

        rows_top10.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_10})
        rows_top100.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_100})
        rows_softmax.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    if out_prefix == "B2":
        pd.DataFrame(rows_top10).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(rows_top100).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2-top100.csv"), index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(rows_softmax).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)
    else:
        pd.DataFrame(rows_top100).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "validation-top100.csv"), index=False)
        pd.DataFrame(rows_softmax).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "validation_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

print("Running TTA for Test set...")
generate_outputs(test_loader, "B2")
print("Running TTA for Validation set...")
generate_outputs(val_loader, "validation")
print("Done. All files saved.")

[Worker-0] 生成的训练代码为:

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.optim as optim
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
def set_seed(seed):
    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
set_seed(SEED)

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_0/round_0/"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

MAX_LEN = 50
D_MODEL = 64
N_HEADS = 4
N_LAYERS = 2
DROPOUT = 0.2
BATCH_SIZE = 256
LR = 1e-3
MAX_EPOCHS = 200
PATIENCE = 40
TTA_ROUNDS = 30

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

all_iids = item_df["iid"].astype(str).unique()
item2idx = {"<pad>": 0}
for iid in all_iids:
    if iid not in item2idx:
        item2idx[iid] = len(item2idx)
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    s = str(s).strip().strip('[]"\'')
    parts = s.replace(',', ' ').split()
    return [p.strip() for p in parts if p.strip()]

def seq_to_idx(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq if x in item2idx]

train_df["seq_idx"] = train_df["item_seq_dedup"].apply(parse_seq).apply(seq_to_idx)
train_df["target_idx"] = train_df["target_iid"].astype(str).map(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
test_df["seq_idx"] = test_df["item_seq_dedup"].apply(parse_seq).apply(seq_to_idx)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df):
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df["uid"].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df["uid"].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df["seq_idx"].tolist()
        self.targets = df["target_idx"].tolist() if not is_test else [0]*len(df)
        self.is_test = is_test

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.uids)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = str(self.uids[idx])
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        target = self.targets[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        pad_len = MAX_LEN - len(seq)
        seq = [0]*pad_len + seq
        mask = [0]*pad_len + [1]*len(seq)
        return uid, torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long), torch.tensor(mask, dtype=torch.long), torch.tensor(target, dtype=torch.long)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
test_ds = RecDataset(test_df, is_test=True)

train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
test_loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model, n_heads, n_layers, dropout):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([
            nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4,
                                       dropout=dropout, batch_first=True, activation='gelu')
            for _ in range(n_layers)
        ])
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
        self.register_buffer("causal_mask", torch.triu(torch.full((max_len, max_len), float('-inf')), diagonal=1))

    def forward(self, seq):
        B, L = seq.size()
        pos = torch.arange(L, device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, L)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        pad_mask = (seq == 0)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x, mask=self.causal_mask[:L, :L], src_key_padding_mask=pad_mask)
        x = self.norm(x)
        return x

model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN, D_MODEL, N_HEADS, N_LAYERS, DROPOUT).cuda()
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4)
scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=MAX_EPOCHS)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience_cnt = 0
best_model_path = os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth")

def compute_ndcg10(model, loader):
    model.eval()
    ndcg_sum = 0.0
    count = 0
    with torch.no_grad():
        for uids, seqs, masks, targets in loader:
            seqs, targets = seqs.cuda(), targets.cuda()
            out = model(seqs)
            valid_lens = torch.clamp(masks.sum(dim=1) - 1, min=0)
            batch_idx = torch.arange(seqs.size(0), device=seqs.device)
            last_out = out[batch_idx, valid_lens]
            logits = last_out @ model.item_emb.weight.T
            logits[:, 0] = -1e9
            _, topk = logits.topk(10, dim=1)
            for i in range(seqs.size(0)):
                t = targets[i].item()
                if t == 0: continue
                ranks = (topk[i] == t).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
                if len(ranks) > 0:
                    rank = ranks[0].item() + 1
                    ndcg_sum += 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
                count += 1
    return ndcg_sum / max(count, 1)

for epoch in range(MAX_EPOCHS):
    model.train()
    epoch_loss = 0.0
    steps = 0
    for uids, seqs, masks, targets in train_loader:
        seqs, targets = seqs.cuda(), targets.cuda()
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        out = model(seqs)
        valid_lens = torch.clamp(masks.sum(dim=1) - 1, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seqs.size(0), device=seqs.device)
        last_out = out[batch_idx, valid_lens]
        logits = last_out @ model.item_emb.weight.T
        loss = criterion(logits, targets)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        epoch_loss += loss.item()
        steps += 1
    scheduler.step()

    val_ndcg = compute_ndcg10(model, val_loader)
    if (epoch + 1) % 10 == 0:
        print(f"Epoch {epoch+1}/{MAX_EPOCHS} | Loss: {epoch_loss/steps:.4f} | Val NDCG@10: {val_ndcg:.4f}")
    
    if val_ndcg > best_ndcg:
        best_ndcg = val_ndcg
        patience_cnt = 0
        torch.save(model.state_dict(), best_model_path)
    else:
        patience_cnt += 1
        if patience_cnt >= PATIENCE:
            print("Early stopping.")
            break

model.load_state_dict(torch.load(best_model_path))
model.train()

def run_tta_inference(loader):
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)
    for _ in range(TTA_ROUNDS):
        with torch.no_grad():
            for uids, seqs, masks, _ in loader:
                seqs = seqs.cuda()
                out = model(seqs)
                valid_lens = torch.clamp(masks.sum(dim=1) - 1, min=0)
                batch_idx = torch.arange(seqs.size(0), device=seqs.device)
                last_out = out[batch_idx, valid_lens]
                logits = last_out @ model.item_emb.weight.T
                probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1).cpu().numpy()
                for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                    uid = str(uid)
                    uid_probs[uid] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid] += 1
    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]
    return uid_probs

print("Running TTA for Validation...")
val_probs = run_tta_inference(val_loader)
print("Running TTA for Test...")
test_probs = run_tta_inference(test_loader)

def generate_outputs(uid_probs, uid_list, prefix):
    rows_top10 = []
    rows_top100 = []
    rows_softmax = []
    for uid in uid_list:
        uid = str(uid)
        scores = uid_probs.get(uid, np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
        valid_indices = np.arange(1, NUM_ITEMS)
        valid_scores = scores[valid_indices]
        sorted_idx = np.argsort(-valid_scores)
        top100_idx = valid_indices[sorted_idx[:100]]
        top100_scores = scores[top100_idx]
        top100_iids = [idx2item[i] for i in top100_idx]
        
        pred10 = ",".join(top100_iids[:10])
        rows_top10.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred10})
        
        pred100 = ",".join(top100_iids)
        rows_top100.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred100})
        
        scores_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{sc:.4f}" for iid, sc in zip(top100_iids, top100_scores)])
        rows_softmax.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": scores_str})

    if prefix == "B2":
        pd.DataFrame(rows_top10).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(rows_top100).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(rows_softmax).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

val_uid_list = sorted(val_split_df["uid"].astype(str).unique())
test_uid_list = sorted(test_df["uid"].astype(str).unique())

generate_outputs(val_probs, val_uid_list, "validation")
generate_outputs(test_probs, test_uid_list, "B2")

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f:
    pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f:
    pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

print("All tasks completed successfully.")

[Worker-2] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：当前核心瓶颈为**严重过拟合与特征利用不足**。Val NDCG在第30轮达峰后持续衰减，表明纯ID序列+SASRec在长序列上发生记忆化，且缺乏正则与数据增强。测试集100%用户冷启动导致User ID失效，但模型未引入高一致性的用户/物品画像特征，泛化力弱。此外，训练序列长且重复度高，测试序列极短（中位数10），固定截断导致分布偏移；调度器T_max与总轮次错位加剧优化停滞。

**调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：采用**SASRec+特征融合**。将8维用户分类特征与物品分类特征经Embedding+MLP交叉后，与Item ID Embedding拼接输入Transformer。降维至dim=64，layers=2，heads=4，抑制过拟合。
2. **序列增强与对齐**：引入**随机裁剪(Random Crop)与动态Mask(prob=0.2)**。训练时聚焦最近30个交互（max_len=30），匹配测试集短序列分布，削弱长程重复噪声。
3. **损失与正则**：替换为**Label Smoothing(0.1)+CE Loss**，配合**流行度去偏负采样**缓解长尾与类别集中偏差。Dropout提至0.3，保留梯度裁剪。
4. **训练策略**：上限Epoch=150，采用**Warmup(5%)+Cosine衰减**，LR=5e-4，BS=256。修复早停机制(eval每5轮，patience=15)，确保在泛化拐点及时保存。预期通过特征补偿冷启动、增强对抗过拟合，稳步突破0.18。

[Worker-1] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：核心阻碍为**100%用户冷启动**与**严重过拟合**。前两轮纯ID-SASRec无法泛化至未见用户，且完全丢弃了高一致性的u_cat/i_cat侧信息；初始LR(1e-3)过高无Warmup，缺乏序列增强，导致模型快速死记训练集，Val NDCG早峰后断崖下跌。此外，训练序列中位数(41)远大于测试集(10)，固定截断加剧分布偏移；高Gini系数与强自转移未做去偏，模型易退化为流行度推荐。

**调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：改用**特征增强SASRec**。物品表征=ID Emb + i_cat_* Emb + 频次位置编码；用户侧将u_cat_*经轻量MLP编码后，与序列末端隐向量Concat送入预测头，彻底解决冷启动泛化。
2. **数据增强**：训练期启用**随机尾部裁剪+随机Mask(prob=0.2)**，强制模型适应短序列；max_len下调至30，匹配测试集近期交互主导特性。
3. **损失与正则**：采用**Sampled Softmax(负采样512) + Label Smoothing(0.1)**抑制头部偏差；优化器改为**Linear Warmup(10轮)+Cosine衰减**，峰值LR降至5e-4；Dropout升至0.3，WD=5e-4，Grad Clip=0.5。
4. **训练配置**：BS=256，Max Epoch=150，Patience=20，每5轮验证。以0.16为基线，通过特征融合与强正则稳步突破泛化瓶颈。

[Worker-2] 生成的训练代码为:
import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_2/round_1"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f:
    pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f:
    pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df):
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 30
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.uids)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=64, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items

    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=5e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=200)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 200

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                    uid_str = str(uid)
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    for uid, probs in uid_probs.items():
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0:
                continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100:
                break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            pred_list = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10_pred = ",".join(pred_list[:10])
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": top10_pred})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")
[Worker-2] 🔥 执行冒烟测试...
[Worker-1] 生成的训练代码为:
import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_1/round_1"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 30
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=5e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=150)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 150

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth")))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                    uid_str = str(uid)
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

[Worker-2] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：历史三轮仅依赖Item ID序列，完全忽略用户/物品类别特征，在测试集100%用户冷启动下缺失关键个性化信号；目标物品基尼系数0.827且类别极度倾斜（Cat4占79%），标准CE损失加剧流行度偏差，长尾召回弱；训练序列中位数41而测试仅10，固定截断引发分布偏移，且Loss持续下降而Val NDCG早衰，暴露严重过拟合、正则不足与调度器错位。

**调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：SASRec+特征融合。Item ID与i_cat/i_bucket嵌入拼接；u_cat特征经轻量MLP编码后以Additive方式注入序列初始表征，弥补冷启动缺陷。
2. **序列增强**：设`max_len=40`。训练期启用动态随机Mask(prob=0.15)与随机尾部截断，模拟测试短序列分布，提升鲁棒性。
3. **损失与采样**：改用Label Smoothing CE(ε=0.1)抑制过拟合；负采样采用“流行度逆频加权+均匀”混合策略，削弱头部偏差，提升长尾排序。
4. **超参调度**：`lr=3e-4`(10轮Warmup)，`wd=5e-4`，`dropout=0.4`，`dim=128`，`bs=256`。对齐`CosineAnnealingLR(T_max=200)`，早停patience=20(每5轮评估)，上限200轮。聚焦泛化与特征利用，稳步突破0.16基线。

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from torch.optim import AdamW
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import CosineAnnealingLR

SEED = 666
def set_seed(seed):
    random.seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
set_seed(SEED)

DATA_DIR = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/'
OUT_DIR = './model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_0/round_0'
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

MAX_SEQ_LEN = 50
EMBED_DIM = 64
NUM_LAYERS = 2
NUM_HEADS = 4
DROPOUT = 0.2
BATCH_SIZE = 256
LR = 1e-3
MAX_EPOCHS = 200
PATIENCE = 40
TTA_ROUNDS = 30
DEVICE = 'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'

def parse_seq(seq_str):
    if pd.isna(seq_str) or str(seq_str).strip() == '':
        return []
    s = str(seq_str).replace(' ', ',').replace(';', ',')
    return [x.strip() for x in re.split(r',+', s) if x.strip()]

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

print("Loading data...")
train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'train.csv'))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'test.csv'))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, 'item.csv'))

all_iids = item_df['iid'].unique().tolist()
item2idx = {iid: i+1 for i, iid in enumerate(all_iids)}
idx2item = {i+1: iid for i, iid in enumerate(all_iids)}
num_items = len(item2idx) + 1

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(lambda x: [item2idx.get(i, 0) for i in x if item2idx.get(i, 0) > 0])
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
train_df = train_df[train_df['target_idx'] > 0].reset_index(drop=True)

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_sub = train_df[~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)].reset_index(drop=True)
val_sub = train_df[train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)].reset_index(drop=True)

test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(lambda x: [item2idx.get(i, 0) for i in x if item2idx.get(i, 0) > 0])

class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.is_test = is_test
        if not is_test:
            self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist()
        else:
            self.targets = [0] * len(df)

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.uids)

    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_SEQ_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_SEQ_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = [0] * (MAX_SEQ_LEN - len(seq)) + seq
        seq_t = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        mask_t = (seq_t > 0).float()
        target_t = torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long)
        return uid, seq_t, mask_t, target_t

def collate_fn(batch):
    uids = [b[0] for b in batch]
    seqs = torch.stack([b[1] for b in batch])
    masks = torch.stack([b[2] for b in batch])
    targets = torch.stack([b[3] for b in batch])
    return uids, seqs, masks, targets

train_loader = DataLoader(RecDataset(train_sub), batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=True, collate_fn=collate_fn, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(RecDataset(val_sub), batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, collate_fn=collate_fn, num_workers=0)
test_loader = DataLoader(RecDataset(test_df, is_test=True), batch_size=BATCH_SIZE, shuffle=False, collate_fn=collate_fn, num_workers=0)

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, embed_dim, max_len, num_layers, num_heads, dropout):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, embed_dim, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, embed_dim)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([
            nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
                d_model=embed_dim, nhead=num_heads, dim_feedforward=embed_dim*4,
                dropout=dropout, batch_first=True, activation='gelu'
            ) for _ in range(num_layers)
        ])
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(embed_dim)
        self.out_proj = nn.Linear(embed_dim, num_items)
        self.register_buffer('pos_ids', torch.arange(max_len).unsqueeze(0))
        self.register_buffer('causal_mask', torch.tril(torch.ones(max_len, max_len)).bool())

    def forward(self, seqs, masks):
        pos = self.pos_ids.expand(seqs.size(0), -1)
        x = self.item_emb(seqs) + self.pos_emb(pos)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        for layer in self.layers:
            x = layer(x, src_key_padding_mask=(masks == 0), mask=self.causal_mask[:seqs.size(1), :seqs.size(1)])
        x = self.norm(x)
        logits = self.out_proj(x)
        return logits

model = SASRec(num_items, EMBED_DIM, MAX_SEQ_LEN, NUM_LAYERS, NUM_HEADS, DROPOUT).to(DEVICE)
optimizer = AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4)
scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=MAX_EPOCHS)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)

def calc_ndcg10(preds, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for p, t in zip(preds, targets):
        if t == 0: continue
        count += 1
        if t in p[:10]:
            rank = p[:10].index(t) + 1
            ndcg += 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience_cnt = 0
print("Start training...")
for epoch in range(1, MAX_EPOCHS + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    steps = 0
    for uids, seqs, masks, targets in train_loader:
        seqs, masks, targets = seqs.to(DEVICE), masks.to(DEVICE), targets.to(DEVICE)
        logits = model(seqs, masks)
        loss = criterion(logits.view(-1, logits.size(-1)), targets.view(-1))
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item()
        steps += 1
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / max(steps, 1)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        val_preds = []
        val_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for uids, seqs, masks, targets in val_loader:
                seqs, masks = seqs.to(DEVICE), masks.to(DEVICE)
                logits = model(seqs, masks)
                probs = F.softmax(logits[:, -1, :], dim=-1)
                _, topk = torch.topk(probs, 10, dim=-1)
                val_preds.extend(topk.cpu().tolist())
                val_targets.extend(targets.tolist())
        ndcg = calc_ndcg10(val_preds, val_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Loss: {avg_loss:.4f} | Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")
        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'best_model.pth'))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 10
        if patience_cnt >= PATIENCE:
            print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
            break

print("Loading best model for TTA...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'best_model.pth')))
model.to(DEVICE)
model.train()

def run_tta_inference(loader, is_test=True):
    uid_probs = {}
    for _ in range(TTA_ROUNDS):
        for uids, seqs, masks, _ in loader:
            seqs, masks = seqs.to(DEVICE), masks.to(DEVICE)
            with torch.no_grad():
                logits = model(seqs, masks)
                probs = F.softmax(logits[:, -1, :], dim=-1).cpu()
            for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                uid = str(uid)
                if uid not in uid_probs:
                    uid_probs[uid] = torch.zeros(num_items)
                uid_probs[uid] += probs[i]
    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= TTA_ROUNDS
    return uid_probs

print("Running TTA for Test set...")
test_uid_probs = run_tta_inference(test_loader, is_test=True)
print("Running TTA for Validation set...")
val_uid_probs = run_tta_inference(val_loader, is_test=False)

def generate_outputs(uid_probs, df_order, prefix, out_dir):
    rows_top10 = []
    rows_top100 = []
    rows_scores = []
    ordered_uids = [str(u) for u in df_order['uid'].unique()]
    for uid in ordered_uids:
        if uid not in uid_probs:
            continue
        probs = uid_probs[uid].clone()
        probs[0] = -1e9
        top100_idx = torch.topk(probs, 100, dim=-1).indices.tolist()
        valid_iids = [idx2item[i] for i in top100_idx if i > 0 and i in idx2item]
        valid_iids = list(dict.fromkeys(valid_iids))
        
        pred_10 = valid_iids[:10]
        pred_100 = valid_iids[:100]
        
        scores_100 = []
        for iid in pred_100:
            idx = item2idx[iid]
            sc = probs[idx].item()
            scores_100.append(f"{iid}:{sc:.4f}")
            
        rows_top10.append({'uid': uid, 'prediction': ','.join(pred_10)})
        rows_top100.append({'uid': uid, 'prediction': ','.join(pred_100)})
        rows_scores.append({'uid': uid, 'item_scores': ','.join(scores_100)})
        
    pd.DataFrame(rows_top10).to_csv(os.path.join(out_dir, f'{prefix}.csv'), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(rows_top100).to_csv(os.path.join(out_dir, f'{prefix}-top100.csv'), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(rows_scores).to_csv(os.path.join(out_dir, f'{prefix}_softmax_item.csv'), index=False)

generate_outputs(test_uid_probs, test_df, 'B2', OUT_DIR)
generate_outputs(val_uid_probs, val_sub, 'validation', OUT_DIR)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'item2idx.pkl'), 'wb') as f:
    pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, 'idx2item.pkl'), 'wb') as f:
    pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

print("All outputs saved successfully.")

[Worker-1] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：当前最佳NDCG@10为0.1662，核心阻碍在于：1）**特征零利用**：测试集100%用户冷启动，但历史实验完全忽略用户画像(u_cat_*)与物品属性(i_cat_*)，纯ID序列建模表征单薄，泛化严重受限；2）**序列分布失配**：训练去重中位长41，测试仅10，固定MAX_LEN=30截断训练信息且未模拟测试短序场景；3）**流行度偏差与优化局限**：目标Gini达0.827，全量CE损失加剧头部物品过拟合，缺乏负采样与平滑正则，导致验证集Epoch 90后快速 plateau。

**调优方案**：1）**架构融合**：保留SASRec(dim=128,layers=2,heads=4)，物品端拼接ID与i_cat_*嵌入；用户端将u_cat_*经轻量MLP编码为全局上下文向量，与序列最终表征相加，弥补冷启动缺失。2）**序列增强**：MAX_LEN=50，训练期引入动态随机裁剪(采样长度10~50)与随机Mask(prob=0.2)，强制模型适配短序分布。3）**损失与超参**：改用SampledSoftmax(neg=256)替代全量CE，配合LabelSmoothing(0.1)与流行度逆频加权抑制头部偏差。优化器AdamW(lr=3e-4,wd=1e-3,dropout=0.4)，调度器采用10%Warmup+Cosine衰减，max_epoch=150，patience=20。预期突破0.18。

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import warnings
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_2/round_2"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = set(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(sorted(valid_iids), 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx[x] for x in seq if x in item2idx]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(df):
    unique_uids = sorted(df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 40
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.4):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=200)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 30
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 200

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth")))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                    uid = str(uid)
                    uid_probs[uid] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []
    b2_rows = []

    for uid, probs in uid_probs.items():
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item.get(idx)
            if iid is None: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

        if is_test:
            b2_pred = ",".join(valid_iids[:10])
            b2_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": b2_pred})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows, columns=["uid", "prediction"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows, columns=["uid", "item_scores"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)
    if is_test:
        pd.DataFrame(b2_rows, columns=["uid", "prediction"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_1/round_2"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0)).astype(int)
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 50
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=150)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 150

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i in range(len(uids)):
                    uid_str = str(uids[i])
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

  "best_model_dir": "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_2/round_2/best_model.pth",

[Worker-2] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：历史NDCG停滞于0.166，核心阻碍有三：1）**特征未利用**：历轮仅用Item ID，完全忽略`u_cat_*`与`i_cat_*`。测试集100%用户冷启动，静态画像与类目特征是泛化关键；2）**序列分布失配**：训练去重中位长41，测试仅10，固定截断30-50导致推理偏移，且无数据增强致使模型死记硬背；3）**流行度偏差**：目标Gini达0.827，自转移极强，标准CE损失无针对性负采样，模型严重偏向头部热门，排序区分度触顶。

**调优方案**：
1. **架构**：特征增强SASRec。Item嵌入拼接`i_cat_*`；`u_cat_*`经轻量MLP编码后与序列最终表征Add融合，弥补冷启动上下文缺失。
2. **序列处理**：`max_len`降至20（聚焦近期交互，贴合测试分布）。训练引入随机尾部裁剪(保留10-20)、随机Mask(prob=0.15)及短序小概率反转。
3. **损失与采样**：改用Label Smoothing CE(ε=0.1)抑制过拟合；负采样采用流行度逆频加权策略，强化中尾部物品判别力。
4. **超参**：`dim=128, layers=2, heads=4, dropout=0.3, wd=1e-4`。AdamW+`lr=3e-4`(Warmup10轮+Cosine衰减)。BS=512，每5轮评估，Patience=15，上限200。聚焦特征融合与分布对齐，目标突破0.18。
