[Worker-1] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：当前NDCG@10停滞于0.17，核心阻碍为：1）**特征完全未用**：历次仅建模Item ID，忽略8维用户画像与4维物品属性。测试集100%用户冷启动，纯ID序列无法跨用户迁移，触及泛化天花板；2）**序列长度失配**：训练去重中位数41，测试仅10。固定截断(MAX_LEN=30~50)使模型过拟合长程依赖，推理时短序列分布偏移严重；3）**流行度偏差**：Gini=0.827且Cat4占79%，标准CE Loss加剧头部主导，长尾排序弱；4）**验证集随机划分**未对齐线上时序，指标提前Plateau。

**本轮调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：采用**特征增强SASRec**。物品侧拼接ID与`i_cat_*`/`i_bucket_01` Embedding；用户侧将`u_cat_*`经轻量MLP编码后，以Additive方式注入序列初始表征，破解冷启动瓶颈。
2. **序列增强**：`MAX_LEN=60`，训练期引入**随机尾部裁剪(p=0.3)与动态Mask(p=0.15)**，强制学习短序列局部模式，对齐测试分布。
3. **损失与采样**：改用**Label Smoothing CE(ε=0.1)**抑制过置信；负采样采用**流行度逆频加权**，提升长尾排序权重。
4. **超参**：`d_model=256, layers=3, lr=2e-4`(Warmup5轮+Cosine), `dropout=0.3, wd=1e-3, bs=512`。Epoch≤200，Patience=15早停。目标突破0.18。
[Worker-1] 🎯 生成优化方向成功: **瓶颈分析**：当前NDCG@10停滞于0.17，核心阻碍为：1）**特征完全未用**：历次仅建模Item ID，忽略8维用户画像与4维物品属性。测试集100%用户冷启动，纯ID序列无法跨用户迁移，触及泛化天花板；2）**序列长度失配**：训练去重中位数41，测试仅10。固定截断(MAX_LEN=30~50)使模型过拟合长程依赖，推理时短序列分布偏移严重；3）**流行度偏差**：Gini=0.827且Cat4占79%，标准CE Loss加剧头部主导，长尾排序弱；4）**验证集随机划分**未对齐线上时序，指标提前Plateau。

**本轮调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：采用**特征增强SASRec**。物品侧拼接ID与`i_cat_*`/`i_bucket_01` Embedding；用户侧将`u_cat_*`经轻量MLP编码后，以Additive方式注入序列初始表征，破解冷启动瓶颈。
2. **序列增强**：`MAX_LEN=60`，训练期引入**随机尾部裁剪(p=0.3)与动态Mask(p=0.15)**，强制学习短序列局部模式，对齐测试分布。
3. **损失与采样**：改用**Label Smoothing CE(ε=0.1)**抑制过置信；负采样采用**流行度逆频加权**，提升长尾排序权重。
4. **超参**：`d_model=256, layers=3, lr=2e-4`(Warmup5轮+Cosine), `dropout=0.3, wd=1e-3, bs=512`。Epoch≤200，Patience=15早停。目标突破0.18。
[Worker-2] 生成的训练代码为:
import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
torch.set_num_threads(1)

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_2/round_3"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"), dtype={"uid": str})
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"), dtype={"uid": str})
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"), dtype={"iid": str})
user_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "user.csv"), dtype={"uid": str})

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0))
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

u_cat_cols = [c for c in user_df.columns if c.startswith('u_cat')]
user_feat_map = {}
for col in u_cat_cols:
    vals = user_df[col].dropna().unique()
    user_feat_map[col] = {v: i+1 for i, v in enumerate(vals)}
    user_feat_map[col]['<unk>'] = 0

def get_user_feats(uid):
    row = user_df[user_df['uid'] == uid]
    if row.empty:
        return [0]*len(u_cat_cols)
    feats = []
    for col in u_cat_cols:
        v = row[col].values[0]
        feats.append(user_feat_map[col].get(v, 0))
    return feats

train_df['u_feats'] = train_df['uid'].apply(get_user_feats)
test_df['u_feats'] = test_df['uid'].apply(get_user_feats)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 20
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.u_feats = df['u_feats'].tolist()
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        u_f = torch.tensor(self.u_feats[idx], dtype=torch.long)
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, u_f, uid
        return seq_tensor, u_f, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3, u_feat_dim=8):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.u_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(u_feat_dim, d_model),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        )
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq, u_feats):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        u_rep = self.u_mlp(u_feats.float())
        seq_rep = seq_rep + u_rep
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3, u_feat_dim=8).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=200)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 200

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, u_f, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, u_f, target = seq.to(device), u_f.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq, u_f)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, u_f, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, u_f, target = seq.to(device), u_f.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq, u_f)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth")))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, u_f, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, u_f, _, uids = batch
                seq, u_f = seq.to(device), u_f.to(device)
                logits = model(seq, u_f)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i, uid in enumerate(uids):
                    uid_str = str(uid)
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_1/round_3"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))
user_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "user.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

user_feat_cols = ['u_cat_01', 'u_cat_02', 'u_cat_03', 'u_cat_04', 'u_cat_05', 'u_cat_06', 'u_cat_07', 'u_cat_08']
user_feat_maps = {}
for col in user_feat_cols:
    vals = train_df[col].astype(str).unique()
    mapping = {v: i+1 for i, v in enumerate(vals)}
    mapping['<pad>'] = 0
    user_feat_maps[col] = mapping

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0)).astype(int)
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 60
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
        self.user_feats = []
        for _, row in df.iterrows():
            feats = []
            for col in user_feat_cols:
                val = str(row.get(col, '<pad>'))
                feats.append(user_feat_maps[col].get(val, 0))
            self.user_feats.append(feats)

    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        u_feat = self.user_feats[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        return (
            torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long),
            torch.tensor(u_feat, dtype=torch.long),
            torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long) if self.targets is not None else torch.tensor(0, dtype=torch.long),
            uid
        )

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=256, n_heads=4, n_layers=3, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.user_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(8, d_model),
            nn.LayerNorm(d_model),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout)
        )
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
        self.max_len = max_len

    def forward(self, seq, u_feat):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        u_vec = self.user_mlp(u_feat.float())
        x = x + u_vec.unsqueeze(1)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=200)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 15
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 200

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, u_feat, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, u_feat, target = seq.to(device), u_feat.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq, u_feat)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, u_feat, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, u_feat, target = seq.to(device), u_feat.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq, u_feat)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                seq, u_feat, _, uids = batch
                seq, u_feat = seq.to(device), u_feat.to(device)
                logits = model(seq, u_feat)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i in range(len(uids)):
                    uid_str = str(uids[i])
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

[Worker-0] 生成的优化方向为: 当前性能瓶颈核心在于：1）**特征未利用**：历次实验仅用Item ID，完全忽略0缺失率的u_cat_*与i_cat_*特征，在测试集100%用户冷启动下严重制约泛化；2）**序列长度失配**：训练去重中位数41，测试仅10，固定max_len=30~50引入历史噪声，模型过拟合长程依赖导致线上线下分布偏移；3）**长尾与流行度偏差**：Gini达0.827且类目4占79%，标准CE损失使模型偏向头部物品，缺乏对长尾目标的判别力；4）**正则与增强不足**：无标签平滑与掩码增强，验证指标在0.17 plateau后衰退。

本轮调优方案：
1）**架构微调**：保留SASRec基座，引入侧特征融合。将u_cat_*嵌入经轻量MLP交叉后与序列池化向量拼接，物品端融合i_cat_*嵌入，补齐冷启动与上下文表征。
2）**序列适配与增强**：max_len降至20对齐测试短序列分布；训练期引入动态随机截断与15%概率Token Mask，提升分布鲁棒性。
3）**损失与超参优化**：改用Label Smoothing CE(ε=0.1)抑制流行度过拟合，配合Popularity-aware负采样加权。设lr=2e-4(含10% Warmup)+Cosine衰减，dropout=0.4，wd=5e-4，d_model=128，bs=256，早停patience=30。聚焦细粒度调优，目标NDCG@10突破0.18。

  "model_and_params": "架构：SASRec(单向因果Transformer)；验证集划分：固定种子随机用户抽样(80/20)；数据增强：训练期无，推理期采用Dropout TTA(30轮)；超参：lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-4, dropout=0.3, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, MAX_LEN=20, batch_size=512；用户特征：8维类别特征经双层MLP映射后与序列表征相加；调度器：CosineAnnealingLR(T_max=200)；损失与正则：CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)+梯度裁剪(max_norm=1.0)+权重衰减。",
  "training_diagnosis": "Loss持续平稳下降至5.49，Val NDCG@10于Epoch 150达峰值0.1661后进入平台期并微幅震荡，未触发早停。训练后期Loss仍降但指标停滞，呈现轻微过拟合/表征饱和迹象。学习率衰减合理，无剧烈震荡。绝对指标偏低，提示序列截断(MAX_LEN=20)可能丢失长程依赖，且交叉熵损失在推荐场景下区分度有限，验证集与线上指标可能存在因负样本分布差异导致的落差隐患。",
  "conclusion_and_next": "结论：良。模型收敛稳定但绝对性能偏低。下轮建议：1.放宽MAX_LEN或引入动态截断；2.替换为BPR/InfoNCE损失或引入难负样本采样；3.微调dropout与学习率预热策略，提升泛化与排序区分度。"

[Worker-2] 🎉 刷新私有最佳 NDCG@10: 0.1661
[Worker-2] 🧠 生成优化方向...
[Worker-0] 生成的训练代码为:
import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import warnings
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_0/round_1"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0)).astype(int)
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 20
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.4):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0, label_smoothing=0.1)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=150)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 30
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 150

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i in range(len(uids)):
                    uid_str = str(uids[i])
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item.get(idx)
            if iid is None: continue
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")
[Worker-0] 🔥 执行冒烟测试...

本次调优策略：
1. **序列适配与增强**：沿用SASRec，设`max_len=50`。训练期引入**动态随机截断**（长度10~50均匀采样）对齐测试分布；叠加15%随机Mask与尾部裁剪，提升短序列鲁棒性。
2. **特征融合**：物品ID Embedding与`i_cat_*`类别Embedding拼接；用户8维特征经双层MLP映射后与序列末位隐向量相加，强化画像引导。
3. **损失与采样**：改用**InfoNCE对比损失**，配合**流行度逆频负采样**（压制头部偏差，激活长尾）；辅以Label Smoothing(0.1)防过置信。

# Feature extraction
item_feat_cols = [c for c in item_df.columns if c.startswith('i_cat_') or c.startswith('i_bucket_')]
user_feat_cols = [c for c in train_df.columns if c.startswith('u_cat_')]

item_feat2idx = {}
item_feat_dims = {}
for col in item_feat_cols:
    vals = item_df[col].astype(str).unique()
    item_feat2idx[col] = {v: i+1 for i, v in enumerate(vals)}
    item_feat2idx[col]['<pad>'] = 0
    item_feat_dims[col] = len(item_feat2idx[col])

user_feat2idx = {}
user_feat_dims = {}
for col in user_feat_cols:
    vals = train_df[col].astype(str).unique()
    user_feat2idx[col] = {v: i+1 for i, v in enumerate(vals)}
    user_feat2idx[col]['<pad>'] = 0
    user_feat_dims[col] = len(user_feat2idx[col])

# Precompute item features for fast lookup
iid_to_row = item_df.set_index('iid').to_dict('index')
idx_to_item_feats = {}
for idx, iid in idx2item.items():
    if idx == 0:
        idx_to_item_feats[idx] = {col: 0 for col in item_feat_cols}
        continue
    row = iid_to_row.get(iid, {})
    feats = {}
    for col in item_feat_cols:
        feats[col] = item_feat2idx[col].get(str(row.get(col, '')), 0)
    idx_to_item_feats[idx] = feats

# Precompute user features
def get_user_feats(df):
    res = []
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        uf = {}
        for col in user_feat_cols:
            uf[col] = user_feat2idx[col].get(str(row[col]), 0)
        res.append(uf)
    return res

train_df['user_feats'] = get_user_feats(train_df)
test_df['user_feats'] = get_user_feats(test_df)

✅ 替换成功: MAX_LEN = 50
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid -> MAX_LEN = 60
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.user_feats = df['user_feats'].tolist()
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        seq = self.seqs[idx].copy()
        # Random tail truncation p=0.3
        if not self.is_test and random.random() < 0.3 and len(seq) > 1:
            seq = seq[-random.randint(1, len(seq)):]

        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))

        # Dynamic mask p=0.15
        if not self.is_test:
            for i in range(len(seq)):
                if seq[i] != 0 and random.random() < 0.15:
                    seq[i] = 0

        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)

        # Item features
        item_feats = {col: [] for col in item_feat_cols}
        for x in seq:
            for col in item_feat_cols:
                item_feats[col].append(idx_to_item_feats[x][col])
        item_feats_tensor = {col: torch.tensor(v, dtype=torch.long) for col, v in item_feats.items()}

        # User features
        uf = self.user_feats[idx]
        user_feats_tensor = {col: torch.tensor(uf[col], dtype=torch.long) for col in user_feat_cols}

        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, item_feats_tensor, user_feats_tensor, self.uids[idx]
        return seq_tensor, item_feats_tensor, user_feats_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), self.uids[idx]
🔧 执行第 3/6 对替换...
✅ 替换成功: class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits -> class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, item_feat_dims, user_feat_dims, d_model=256, n_heads=4, n_layers=3, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.item_feat_embs = nn.ModuleDict()
        for col, dim in item_feat_dims.items():
            self.item_feat_embs[col] = nn.Embedding(dim, d_model // max(len(item_feat_dims), 1), padding_idx=0)
        self.user_feat_mlp = None
        if user_feat_dims:
            self.user_feat_embs = nn.ModuleDict()
            for col, dim in user_feat_dims.items():
                self.user_feat_embs[col] = nn.Embedding(dim, 16, padding_idx=0)
            self.user_feat_mlp = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(16 * len(user_feat_dims), d_model),
                nn.GELU(),
                nn.Dropout(dropout)
            )
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items

    def forward(self, seq, item_feats, user_feats):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq)

        if item_feats:
            feat_embs = []
            for col in item_feats:
                feat_embs.append(self.item_feat_embs[col](item_feats[col]))
            x = x + sum(feat_embs)

        if user_feats and self.user_feat_mlp:
            u_embs = []
            for col in user_feats:
                u_embs.append(self.user_feat_embs[col](user_feats[col]))
            u_vec = torch.cat(u_embs, dim=-1)
            x = x + self.user_feat_mlp(u_vec).unsqueeze(1)

        x = x + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=150)

val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)

val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0) -> device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN, item_feat_dims, user_feat_dims).to(device)

# Popularity inverse frequency weighting
pop_counts = train_split_df['target_idx'].value_counts().to_dict()
pop_weights = torch.ones(NUM_ITEMS, device=device)
for idx, cnt in pop_counts.items():
    if idx < NUM_ITEMS:
        pop_weights[idx] = 1.0 / (cnt + 1)
pop_weights = pop_weights / pop_weights.sum() * NUM_ITEMS

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(weight=pop_weights, label_smoothing=0.1, ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)

# Warmup 5 + Cosine
warmup_steps = 5
total_steps = 200
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[
    torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=warmup_steps),
    torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=total_steps - warmup_steps)
], milestones=[warmup_steps])

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)

val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

✅ 替换成功: def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 150

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break -> def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):

            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)

        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

✅ 替换成功: def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i in range(len(uids)):
                    uid_str = str(uids[i])
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)

Epoch 110, Loss: 6.7254, Val NDCG@10: 0.1314
[Worker-2] 生成的训练代码为:

import os
import math
import random
import pickle
import re
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import Dataset, DataLoader
from collections import defaultdict

SEED = 666
random.seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False

DATA_DIR = "/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/rec_data/"
OUT_DIR = "./model_save_vb17/rec_model/worker_2/round_4"
os.makedirs(OUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)

train_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "train.csv"))
test_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "test.csv"))
item_df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(DATA_DIR, "item.csv"))

def parse_seq(s):
    if pd.isna(s): return []
    return re.findall(r'i\d+', str(s))

train_df['seq_list'] = train_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)
test_df['seq_list'] = test_df['item_seq_dedup'].apply(parse_seq)

valid_iids = sorted(item_df['iid'].astype(str).unique())
item2idx = {'<pad>': 0}
for i, iid in enumerate(valid_iids, 1):
    item2idx[iid] = i
idx2item = {v: k for k, v in item2idx.items()}
NUM_ITEMS = len(item2idx)

def map_seq(seq):
    return [item2idx.get(x, 0) for x in seq]

train_df['seq_idx'] = train_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)
train_df['target_idx'] = train_df['target_iid'].astype(str).apply(lambda x: item2idx.get(x, 0)).astype(int)
test_df['seq_idx'] = test_df['seq_list'].apply(map_seq)

def _reproduce_val_split(train_df: pd.DataFrame) -> set:
    unique_uids = sorted(train_df["uid"].unique())
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_uids = rng.permutation(unique_uids)
    n_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_uids) * 0.8)
    return set(shuffled_uids[n_train:])

val_uids = _reproduce_val_split(train_df)
train_mask = ~train_df['uid'].isin(val_uids)
train_split_df = train_df[train_mask].copy()
val_split_df = train_df[~train_mask].copy()

MAX_LEN = 50
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=200)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 200

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "item2idx.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(item2idx, f)
with open(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "idx2item.pkl"), "wb") as f: pickle.dump(idx2item, f)

def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i in range(len(uids)):
                    uid_str = str(uids[i])
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item.get(idx)
            if iid is None: continue
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows, columns=["uid", "prediction"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows, columns=["uid", "item_scores"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows, columns=["uid", "prediction"]).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False)

print("Running Validation TTA...")
run_tta_inference(val_split_df, "validation", is_test=False)
print("Running Test TTA...")
run_tta_inference(test_df, "B2", is_test=True)
print("Done.")

class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx]
        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))
        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, uid
        return seq_tensor, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid -> # Feature extraction
i_feat_cols = [c for c in item_df.columns if c.startswith('i_cat_') or c == 'i_bucket_01']
u_feat_cols = [c for c in train_df.columns if c.startswith('u_cat_')]

item_feat_dict = {}
for col in i_feat_cols:
    vals = item_df[col].astype(str).unique()
    item_feat_dict[col] = {v: i+1 for i, v in enumerate(vals)}
    item_feat_dict[col]['<pad>'] = 0

def map_item_feats(row):
    return [item_feat_dict[col].get(str(row[col]), 0) for col in i_feat_cols]
item_df['feat_idx'] = item_df.apply(map_item_feats, axis=1)
item_feat_map = {str(row['iid']): row['feat_idx'] for _, row in item_df.iterrows()}

def map_user_feats(row):
    return [float(row[col]) if pd.notna(row[col]) else 0.0 for col in u_feat_cols]
train_df['u_feat'] = train_df.apply(map_user_feats, axis=1)
test_df['u_feat'] = test_df.apply(map_user_feats, axis=1)

pop_counts = train_df['target_idx'].value_counts().reindex(range(NUM_ITEMS), fill_value=0).values
pop_weights = torch.tensor(1.0 / (pop_counts + 1.0), dtype=torch.float32)

MAX_LEN = 60
class RecDataset(Dataset):
    def __init__(self, df, is_test=False):
        self.uids = df['uid'].astype(str).tolist()
        self.seqs = df['seq_idx'].tolist()
        self.targets = df['target_idx'].tolist() if not is_test else None
        self.u_feats = df['u_feat'].tolist()
        self.is_test = is_test
    def __len__(self): return len(self.uids)
    def __getitem__(self, idx):
        uid = self.uids[idx]
        seq = self.seqs[idx].copy()
        u_feat = torch.tensor(self.u_feats[idx], dtype=torch.float32)

        if not self.is_test:
            if random.random() < 0.3 and len(seq) > 1:
                seq = seq[-random.randint(1, len(seq)):]
            if random.random() < 0.15:
                mask_idx = random.sample(range(len(seq)), max(1, int(len(seq)*0.15)))
                for mi in mask_idx:
                    seq[mi] = 0

        if len(seq) > MAX_LEN:
            seq = seq[-MAX_LEN:]
        else:
            seq = seq + [0] * (MAX_LEN - len(seq))

        seq_tensor = torch.tensor(seq, dtype=torch.long)
        item_feats_seq = []
        for iid_idx in seq:
            if iid_idx == 0:
                item_feats_seq.append([0]*len(i_feat_cols))
            else:
                iid_str = idx2item[iid_idx]
                item_feats_seq.append(item_feat_map.get(iid_str, [0]*len(i_feat_cols)))
        item_feats_tensor = torch.tensor(item_feats_seq, dtype=torch.long)

        if self.is_test:
            return seq_tensor, item_feats_tensor, u_feat, uid
        return seq_tensor, item_feats_tensor, u_feat, torch.tensor(self.targets[idx], dtype=torch.long), uid

✅ 替换成功: class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=128, n_heads=4, n_layers=2, dropout=0.3):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
    def forward(self, seq):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq) + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits -> class SASRec(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_items, max_len, d_model=256, n_heads=4, n_layers=3, dropout=0.3, num_i_feats=0, num_u_feats=0):
        super().__init__()
        self.item_emb = nn.Embedding(num_items, d_model, padding_idx=0)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(max_len, d_model)
        self.emb_dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        
        self.i_feat_embs = nn.ModuleList([nn.Embedding(100, d_model // max(num_i_feats, 1), padding_idx=0) for _ in range(num_i_feats)]) if num_i_feats > 0 else None
        self.i_feat_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model) if num_i_feats > 0 else None
        
        self.u_mlp = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(num_u_feats, d_model),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
        ) if num_u_feats > 0 else None
        
        encoder_layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model=d_model, nhead=n_heads, dim_feedforward=d_model*4, dropout=dropout, batch_first=True)
        self.encoder = nn.TransformerEncoder(encoder_layer, num_layers=n_layers)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        self.num_items = num_items
        
    def forward(self, seq, item_feats, user_feats):
        mask = (seq == 0)
        pos_ids = torch.arange(seq.size(1), device=seq.device).unsqueeze(0).expand_as(seq)
        x = self.item_emb(seq)
        
        if self.i_feat_proj is not None:
            i_embs = [emb(item_feats[:, :, i]) for i, emb in enumerate(self.i_feat_embs)]
            i_feat_sum = torch.stack(i_embs, dim=-1).sum(dim=-1)
            x = x + self.i_feat_proj(i_feat_sum)
            
        x = x + self.pos_emb(pos_ids)
        
        if self.u_mlp is not None:
            u_emb = self.u_mlp(user_feats)
            x = x + u_emb.unsqueeze(1)
            
        x = self.emb_dropout(x)
        sz = seq.size(1)
        causal_mask = torch.triu(torch.ones(sz, sz, device=seq.device), diagonal=1).bool()
        out = self.encoder(x, mask=causal_mask, src_key_padding_mask=mask)
        lengths = (~mask).sum(dim=1) - 1
        lengths = torch.clamp(lengths, min=0)
        batch_idx = torch.arange(seq.size(0), device=seq.device)
        seq_rep = out[batch_idx, lengths]
        seq_rep = self.dropout(seq_rep)
        logits = torch.matmul(seq_rep, self.item_emb.weight.t())
        return logits

model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=150)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

def compute_ndcg10(pred_indices, targets):
    ndcg = 0.0
    count = 0
    for i in range(len(targets)):
        t = targets[i]
        if t == 0: continue
        preds = pred_indices[i]
        hit_idx = np.where(preds == t)[0]
        if len(hit_idx) > 0:
            rank = hit_idx[0] + 1
            dcg = 1.0 / math.log2(rank + 1)
            idcg = 1.0 / math.log2(2)
            ndcg += dcg / idcg
        count += 1
    return ndcg / max(count, 1)

best_ndcg = -1.0
patience = 40
patience_cnt = 0
max_epochs = 150

for epoch in range(1, max_epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    total_loss = 0.0
    for seq, target, _ in train_loader:
        seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
        optimizer.zero_grad()
        logits = model(seq)
        loss = criterion(logits, target)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), max_norm=1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        total_loss += loss.item() * seq.size(0)
    scheduler.step()
    avg_loss = total_loss / len(train_ds)

    if epoch % 10 == 0:
        model.eval()
        all_preds = []
        all_targets = []
        with torch.no_grad():
            for seq, target, _ in val_loader:
                seq, target = seq.to(device), target.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                _, top10 = torch.topk(logits, 10, dim=1)
                all_preds.append(top10.cpu().numpy())
                all_targets.append(target.cpu().numpy())
        all_preds = np.concatenate(all_preds, axis=0)
        all_targets = np.concatenate(all_targets, axis=0)
        ndcg = compute_ndcg10(all_preds, all_targets)
        print(f"Epoch {epoch}, Loss: {avg_loss:.4f}, Val NDCG@10: {ndcg:.4f}")

        if ndcg > best_ndcg:
            best_ndcg = ndcg
            patience_cnt = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"))
        else:
            patience_cnt += 1
            if patience_cnt >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break -> device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
model = SASRec(NUM_ITEMS, MAX_LEN, d_model=256, n_heads=4, n_layers=3, dropout=0.3, num_i_feats=len(i_feat_cols), num_u_feats=len(u_feat_cols)).to(device)
criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(ignore_index=0, label_smoothing=0.1, weight=pop_weights.to(device))
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)

warmup_epochs = 5
total_epochs = 200
def lr_lambda(epoch):
    if epoch < warmup_epochs:
        return (epoch + 1) / warmup_epochs
    return 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * (epoch - warmup_epochs) / (total_epochs - warmup_epochs)))
scheduler = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(optimizer, lr_lambda)

train_ds = RecDataset(train_split_df)
val_ds = RecDataset(val_split_df)
train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=512, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)

                break

✅ 替换成功: def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):
    ds = RecDataset(df, is_test=is_test)
    loader = DataLoader(ds, batch_size=256, shuffle=False, num_workers=0)
    model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "best_model.pth"), map_location=device))
    model.train()

    tta_rounds = 30
    uid_probs = defaultdict(lambda: np.zeros(NUM_ITEMS))
    uid_counts = defaultdict(int)

    with torch.no_grad():
        for _ in range(tta_rounds):
            for batch in loader:
                if is_test:
                    seq, uids = batch
                else:
                    seq, _, uids = batch
                seq = seq.to(device)
                logits = model(seq)
                probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()
                for i in range(len(uids)):
                    uid_str = str(uids[i])
                    uid_probs[uid_str] += probs[i]
                    uid_counts[uid_str] += 1

    for uid in uid_probs:
        uid_probs[uid] /= uid_counts[uid]

    top100_rows = []
    softmax_rows = []

    sorted_uids = sorted(uid_probs.keys())
    for uid in sorted_uids:
        probs = uid_probs[uid]
        top100_idx = np.argsort(probs)[::-1][:100]
        valid_iids = []
        valid_scores = []
        for idx in top100_idx:
            if idx == 0: continue
            iid = idx2item[idx]
            if iid in valid_iids: continue
            valid_iids.append(iid)
            valid_scores.append(probs[idx])
            if len(valid_iids) == 100: break

        pred_str = ",".join(valid_iids)
        score_str = ",".join([f"{iid}:{score:.4f}" for iid, score in zip(valid_iids, valid_scores)])

        top100_rows.append({"uid": uid, "prediction": pred_str})
        softmax_rows.append({"uid": uid, "item_scores": score_str})

    pd.DataFrame(top100_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}-top100.csv"), index=False)
    pd.DataFrame(softmax_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, f"{out_prefix}_softmax_item.csv"), index=False)

    if is_test:
        top10_rows = []
        for r in top100_rows:
            preds = r["prediction"].split(",")
            top10 = preds[:10]
            top10_rows.append({"uid": r["uid"], "prediction": ",".join(top10)})
        pd.DataFrame(top10_rows).to_csv(os.path.join(OUT_DIR, "B2.csv"), index=False) -> def run_tta_inference(df, out_prefix, is_test=False):

[Worker-0] 生成的优化方向为: **瓶颈分析**：当前Val NDCG卡在0.17的核心症结在于**100%用户冷启动划分**与**纯ID序列建模**的冲突。验证集按UID随机切分，导致验证用户训练期完全未见，SASRec仅依赖物品ID无法向新用户泛化。其次，训练序列中位数(41)显著长于测试集(10)，固定截断造成分布偏移；标准CE Loss未针对高长尾分布(Gini=0.827)进行难负样本挖掘，排序区分度触顶。

**调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：构建**特征增强SASRec**。Item侧将ID Embedding与i_cat_*特征拼接；User侧构建轻量MLP提取u_cat_*表征，通过Gate机制与序列末端输出融合，直接破解冷启动泛化瓶颈。
2. **序列处理与增强**：设`MAX_LEN=30`，训练期采用**动态右截断+随机Mask(15%)+随机裁剪**，对齐测试短序列分布。移除推理TTA，确保评估确定性。
3. **损失与采样**：替换CE为**InfoNCE Loss**，采用**In-batch负采样+流行度逆频加权采样**，压制头部偏差，提升长尾物品召回。配合Label Smoothing(0.1)。
4. **超参配置**：`d_model=128, n_layers=2, n_heads=4, lr=2e-4(Warmup 10%+Cosine), dropout=0.3, wd=5e-4, bs=512`。Epoch≤200，Patience=30。聚焦特征交叉与对比学习，稳步突破0.17天花板。

**调优方案**：
1. **架构升级**：构建**特征增强SASRec**。Item侧将ID Embedding与i_cat_*特征拼接；User侧构建轻量MLP提取u_cat_*表征，通过Gate机制与序列末端输出融合，直接破解冷启动泛化瓶颈。
2. **序列处理与增强**：设`MAX_LEN=30`，训练期采用**动态右截断+随机Mask(15%)+随机裁剪**，对齐测试短序列分布。移除推理TTA，确保评估确定性。
3. **损失与采样**：替换CE为**InfoNCE Loss**，采用**In-batch负采样+流行度逆频加权采样**，压制头部偏差，提升长尾物品召回。配合Label Smoothing(0.1)。
4. **超参配置**：`d_model=128, n_layers=2, n_heads=4, lr=2e-4(Warmup 10%+Cosine), dropout=0.3, wd=5e-4, bs=512`。Epoch≤200，Patience=30。聚焦特征交叉与对比学习，稳步突破0.17天花板。
